     1	\section{Load Dependence}
     2	\label{sec:load}
     3	
     4	A negative result about adaptivity invites the obvious objection: adaptivity
     5	pays when conditions \emph{change}, and a single stationary trace is the one
     6	case where a static cap should be near-optimal. We agree. What we test here is
     7	the weaker thing---two separate constant-load tiers---and we also ran the
     8	stronger one, a trace whose load moves mid-run, whose baselines co-tenancy made
     9	unusable; \S\ref{sec:discussion} reports both what this section leaves open and
    10	why that experiment has no result to give.
    11	
    12	We run the same controller and the same cap family on a 25-session tier. The
    13	throttle configuration is reversed: with all safety throttles disabled, the
    14	25-session tier runs \emph{51\% faster} (58.4 to 28.8 minutes) at a cost of
    15	only 2.4\,pp of SLO, while on the 200-session tier disabling the same
    16	throttles costs 42\% (291.3 to 415.0 minutes) and 43.7\,pp of SLO. One switch,
    17	two regimes, opposite signs, both around 40--50\%
    18	(Fig.~\ref{fig:loadtier}). No single scalar setting is optimal across both.
    19	
    20	We flag the generation gap rather than let the figure imply more than we ran.
    21	The two 200-session numbers are current; the two 25-session numbers are from an
    22	earlier harness revision, and we never ran the all-throttles-off arm on the
    23	25-session tier under the current one. So the reversal is a direction we
    24	measured across two harness generations, not a same-generation paired
    25	comparison, and a reviewer should read it that way. The same-generation
    26	25-session measurement we do have is in Table~\ref{tab:tier25}, and it does not
    27	contain that arm.
    28	
    29	This is the regime in which adaptive control should win, and we now have the
    30	same-generation measurement that earlier drafts of this paper were missing.
    31	Four arms on the 25-session tier, all under the current harness
    32	(Table~\ref{tab:tier25}): static caps of two, three and four finish in 32.4,
    33	24.2 and 23.6 minutes at 92.8\%, 91.2\% and 79.2\% SLO, and \sys{} finishes
    34	in 27.6 minutes at 92.8\%.
    35	
    36	\begin{table}[tb]
    37	  \caption{The 25-session tier, all four arms under the current harness.
    38	  This is the same-generation comparison earlier drafts of this paper were
    39	  missing; \texttt{cap4} and the \sys{} arm were run for this revision. The
    40	  segment SD here is over six segments, not the ten Table~\ref{tab:runs} uses
    41	  for 999-step runs, so the two tables' SD columns are not comparable.}
    42	  \label{tab:tier25}
    43	  \centering
    44	  \small
    45	  \setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}
    46	  \begin{tabular}{lrrrr}
    47	    \toprule
    48	    Policy & Wall (min) & Conc. & SLO (\%) & Seg.\ SD (pp) \\
    49	    \midrule
    50	    cap2          & 32.4 & 1.95 & 92.8 & 11.0 \\
    51	    cap3          & 24.2 & 2.77 & 91.2 &  5.1 \\
    52	    cap4          & 23.6 & 3.39 & 79.2 & 21.4 \\
    53	    \sys{}        & 27.6 & 2.47 & 92.8 &  7.1 \\
    54	    \bottomrule
    55	  \end{tabular}
    56	\end{table}
    57	
    58	The finding is a trade, not a defeat, and it is the opposite of what the
    59	200-session tier shows. Against the same-generation envelope---which runs from
    60	\texttt{cap4} at (23.6, 79.2) through \texttt{cap3} at (24.2, 91.2) to
    61	\texttt{cap2} at (32.4, 92.8)---\sys{} sits $+0.9$\,pp above the line. A
    62	static cap of three is still 12\% faster in wall clock, but it gives up
    63	1.6\,pp of SLO to do it, so neither dominates the other. On this tier the
    64	controller is a different point on the front rather than a dominated one,
    65	which is exactly what the 200-session tier denies it.
    66	
    67	We are careful about the size, and about the yardstick. $+0.9$\,pp is
    68	measured against a straight-line interpolation between static points, not
    69	against a switching policy anyone ran, so the defensible statement is that
    70	\sys{} is not dominated by any static point we measured on this tier---not
    71	that it beats what a static mix could achieve. On 125 requests one request is
    72	worth 0.8\,pp, so the margin is about one request. The tier's
    73	segment-to-segment SD (5.1 to 21.4\,pp) is not the right yardstick: segment
    74	volatility is not run-to-run uncertainty. The right one is the replicate spread
    75	we have---which, across the five configurations we repeated, is 0.5, 0.8, 1.0,
    76	3.9 and 5.1\,pp (\texttt{replicates.csv}). Against the two tightest of those $+0.9$\,pp is about a replicate
    77	wide; against the three widest---which belong to the configurations that decide
    78	our headline elsewhere---it is inside one. We quote the tightest pair
    79	because it is the least favourable to us, not because it is the whole
    80	distribution. What we will defend is the weaker claim: on the
    81	low-load tier the controller is not worse, and the same switch that is worth
    82	$+51\%$ there costs $-42\%$ at high load. A controller that is competitive
    83	where load is light and dominated where load is heavy is the pattern this
    84	paper set out to explain, and it is the pattern we measured.
    85	
    86	The second finding is the more useful one. The signal an adaptive controller
    87	would need is available---the number of sessions waiting for admission is four
    88	times larger on the 200-session tier than on the same-generation 25-session arm
    89	(median 66 against 16, maximum 135 against 25; the earlier-harness 25-session
    90	arms of \S\ref{sec:load} read 9--11, which is where the 6--7$\times$ of an
    91	earlier draft came from, and we quote the same-generation pair)---but its
    92	\emph{interpretation} is not obvious. At the 25-session tier the controller
    93	holds sixteen sessions back while running one; that backlog is manufactured by
    94	the throttling itself rather than
    95	evidence that demand has slackened. Reading it correctly requires combining
    96	instantaneous backlog with progress through the run, which is a stateful
    97	computation a cap cannot perform. We regard this as the most promising
    98	direction we did not finish, and we are explicit that we did not finish it.
    99	
   100	\begin{figure}[tb]
   101	  \centering
   102	  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig5_loadtier.pdf}
   103	  \caption{The same throttle switch, two load tiers, opposite signs. The
   104	  25-session bars are from an earlier harness revision and are shown as a
   105	  direction, not a magnitude---the current-harness 25-session comparison is in
   106	  Table~\ref{tab:tier25} instead. The 200-session bars are current. The two
   107	  panels have independent axes, so the bars are not comparable in height
   108	  across panels---the annotations give each panel's own change.}
   109	  \label{fig:loadtier}
   110	\end{figure}
   111	
   112	\section{Related Work}
   113	\label{sec:related}
   114	
   115	\paragraph{Admission and scheduling for agentic serving.} Recent systems
   116	report large gains from treating an agent session, rather than a request, as
   117	the schedulable unit, and from preserving session state across tool calls:
   118	session-aware admission with CPU-restorable KV
   119	\cite{talaria,continuum}, program-level pause/resume with shortest-context-first
   120	restoration \cite{thunderagent}, and congestion-based control over an
   121	agent-level window \cite{concur}. A recurring measurement in this line is that
   122	recomputation, not admission, dominates: \cite{concur} attributes 49.1\% of
   123	end-to-end latency to recomputation, and \cite{mori} preserves state on the
   124	CPU at the cost of a reload. Our results are consistent with this literature
   125	rather than in tension with it, and \S\ref{sec:discussion} explains why: the
   126	mechanism these systems add is one our controller already has.
   127	
   128	\paragraph{Engine-side techniques.} A separate line improves the engine
   129	rather than the admission decision. Iteration-level batching and paged KV
   130	\cite{yu2022orca,kwon2023pagedattention} are the substrate every policy here runs
   131	on; chunked prefill with stall-free batching \cite{sarathi}, prefill/decode
   132	disaggregation \cite{distserve}, and cache-centric scheduling
   133	\cite{mooncake} are the refinements. Our \S\ref{sec:model} measures 2.6--8.2\,ms of each
   134	27--37\,ms step as work that is not decoding---prefill and non-decode
   135	overhead---which is exactly what this line attacks. We expect these techniques to move the frontier rather than to decide who
   136	sits above it, but we did not run the interaction and so state it as an
   137	expectation.
   138	
   139	\paragraph{State retention and preemption.} A third line keeps session state
   140	alive across the gaps we measure in \S\ref{sec:model}: keeping KV on cheaper
   141	storage across turns \cite{yu2025pensieve,gao2024cachedattention}, migrating it
   142	between instances \cite{sun2024llumnix}, and preempting at iteration granularity
   143	\cite{wu2026fastserve}. These systems exercise finer control than our 2\,s
   144	admission cycle, which is the boundary of our negative result: we falsify one
   145	control granularity, not the family. What they share with \sys{} is that the
   146	state is not discarded---which is why, as we argue below, adding admission
   147	intelligence on top buys nothing here.
   148	
   149	\paragraph{Application-level dependencies and replay.} Serving an LLM
   150	\emph{application} rather than a request means the scheduler sees dependencies
   151	between calls \cite{lin2024parrot}, and agentic traces have structure that
   152	request-level benchmarks do not capture \cite{wang2026xperf}. Our replay
   153	preserves that structure but fixes it in advance, which is exactly the
   154	limitation these works would press on.
   155	
   156	\paragraph{Prefix caching.} The prefix cache is often proposed as the signal
   157	an admission policy should use. What we measure is that the \emph{realized}
   158	reuse is a function of the admission policy and of the host tier, while the
   159	\emph{planned} reuse is a constant of the trace; an earlier draft of this
   160	paper conflated the two and got the direction wrong in both. The trace plans
   161	a round-1-and-later hit rate of 80.9\%. Without HiCache the realized
   162	round-1+ rate follows the plan only at the loosest cap and then collapses:
   163	80.8\%, 78.5\%, 70.6\%, 56.0\%, 32.8\% for caps of one through five. With
   164	HiCache it tracks the plan at every cap we ran: 80.7\%, 79.6\%, 78.7\% for
   165	caps of two, three and four. Our controller sits at 80.6\%.
   166	So the honest statement is not that admission does not move this quantity---%
   167	\texttt{cap4} alone moves it 25 points---but that the host tier is what keeps
   168	it from moving, which is the same mechanism \S\ref{sec:model} and
   169	\S\ref{sec:criterion} keep running into. A configuration without a host tier
   170	that admits four sessions does not have the cache that a host-tier one at the
   171	same concurrency does. All eight numbers in this paragraph are the
   172	\emph{observed} per-step hit rate (\texttt{server\_prefix\_hit\_rate} in the
   173	per-step logs), unweighted mean over rounds $\ge 1$; the planned rate is the
   174	trace constant 80.9\% and does not move with the cap. \S\ref{sec:model} uses
   175	a different convention---all steps, token-weighted, so that reuse and
   176	recomputation partition the prompt---under which the same collapse reads
   177	92.5\%$\to$36.4\% of the trace's bound for the device-only caps and
   178	92.4\%$\to$90.2\% for the static host-tier caps. We state both because the paper's earlier
   179	drafts mixed them. We note the boundary of this claim---we varied admission policy,
   180	not eviction policy, and SGLang exposes alternate eviction policies we did not
   181	test. A characterization of production KV-cache traffic at a large cloud
   182	provider finds that eviction-policy design is ``highly workload-dependent''
   183	and that reuse patterns differ sharply by request category
   184	\cite{wang2025kvcachewild}; that is reason to expect a single trace family to
   185	understate the space, though it is not a policy comparison and we do not
   186	present it as one.
   187	
   188	\section{Discussion}
   189	\label{sec:discussion}
   190	
   191	\paragraph{Why published gains do not reappear.} The systems cited above win
   192	by keeping session state alive across tool waits and by pausing at agent
   193	granularity rather than request granularity. \sys{} has both properties by
   194	construction: the permit file gives per-session pause and resume, and the
   195	controller never frees capacity by discarding a session---it admits fewer
   196	sessions instead. To that extent the mechanism these systems add is one our
   197	controller already has, which is one reason adding admission intelligence on
   198	top of it buys nothing here.
   199	
   200	We are careful not to overstate this, because the distinction is between
   201	\emph{choosing} to discard state and \emph{having} it evicted. Retention here
   202	is the engine's radix cache doing its ordinary job; it is not something the
   203	controller implements, and \S\ref{sec:model} measures that the cache evicts
   204	parked prefixes under pressure and that they are recomputed. What \sys{}
   205	avoids is the deliberate trade of state for capacity. A system that manages the
   206	host tier as an explicit policy---as \cite{mori} and \cite{yu2025pensieve}
   207	do---holds a lever we do not, and our negative result does not cover it.
   208	
   209	\paragraph{The threshold decides the verdict, and we report that.} Every SLO
   210	number in this paper counts a request as good if its time-to-first-token is
   211	under 10\,s. That constant sets the whole vertical axis, so we re-derived the
   212	frontier at 5\,s and 20\,s as well, from the same per-request logs. The
   213	ordering of the static caps does not change. What changes is where the
   214	controllers sit relative to them, and at the loose end one of them crosses:
   215	
   216	\begin{center}
   217	\small
   218	\setlength{\tabcolsep}{4pt}
   219	\begin{tabular}{lrrr}
   220	  \toprule
   221	  SLO threshold & 5\,s & 10\,s & 20\,s \\
   222	  \midrule
   223	  \texttt{horizon}=1        & $-2.9$ & $-0.9$ & $+1.5$ \\
   224	  \sys{} base               & $-2.3$ & $-1.3$ & $+0.6$ \\
   225	  \texttt{bres}             & $-3.6$ & $-1.4$ & $+0.3$ \\
   226	  Concur-style AIMD        & $-14.2$ & $-11.2$ & $-6.5$ \\
   227	  \bottomrule
   228	\end{tabular}
   229	\end{center}
   230	
   231	Two readings, and we give both. The first is that our headline claim is
   232	conditional: at the strict threshold that interactive serving actually cares
   233	about, no controller reaches the static front; at a loose one, one does. The
   234	second is that the crossing is still not a win, though the reason is more
   235	awkward than we first wrote. $+1.5$\,pp at 20\,s is larger than the replicate
   236	SLO spread of the three tightest pairs we have---0.5\,pp for the base
   237	controller, 0.8\,pp for \texttt{cap3}+HiCache and 1.0\,pp for
   238	\texttt{cap2}+HiCache---but smaller than the two widest, 3.9\,pp for the best
   239	arm's own repeat and 5.1\,pp for \texttt{cap4}+HiCache, and those two are
   240	anchors. It is the anchors, not the controllers, that set the line the margin
   241	is measured against, so the comparison that matters is the one against the
   242	widest anchor---where the margin is not resolvable. (An earlier revision discarded
   243	the \texttt{cap3}+HiCache pair on the ground that one run had exceeded its
   244	cap. That was wrong: the over-cap run is the \emph{device-only}
   245	\texttt{cap3} repeat, and both HiCache runs peak at three with none over. The
   246	pair is a valid replicate and we use it, including as an envelope anchor.)
   247	A margin that appears only at one threshold
   248	and only at a size we cannot resolve is not evidence that adaptive admission
   249	works; it is evidence that the question needs more runs than we have. We state
   250	the conditional version in the abstract, and we flag this as the second thing
   251	we would re-run with more GPU time.
   252

     1	# `foyer_bres` 干净重跑落地后的连锁处置（2026-09-25 00:0x）
     2	
     3	争用审计（`docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md`）判定的四条受损控制器臂中，
     4	第一条 `bres` 的干净重跑 `foyer_bres_r2` 已跑完并落地。它的数字**改变了一条结论的推理**，
     5	不只是精度，因此单独记一份。
     6	
     7	## 一、重跑前后的对照（原始日志）
     8	
     9	| | 旧 `foyer_bres`（争用） | 新 `foyer_bres_r2`（干净） |
    10	|---|---|---|
    11	| 墙钟 | 295.47 min | 265.87 min |
    12	| SLO | 83.8 % | 85.9 % |
    13	| Δ（对静态前沿） | −7.81 pp | −1.33 pp |
    14	| τ₂（客户端逐请求 decode 间隔） | 31.3 ms | 24.8 ms |
    15	| 占用中位 | 56.9 % | 56.2 % |
    16	| R̄_busy | 1.481 | 1.481 |
    17	| 复用占 prompt | 0.6943 | 0.6904 |
    18	| round-1+ 命中 | 80.2 % | 79.7 % |
    19	
    20	对照对象是 `foyer_hc`（保留记账的那条基线）：291.28 min、89.79 %、Δ −1.24、τ₂ 23.8 ms、
    21	占用 49.2 %、R̄ 1.353。
    22	
    23	**体积列几乎不动（复用差 0.39 点），时间列动 10 %** —— 这是「争用只在客户端时钟上露馅」
    24	这句话最直接的证据，已经写进 §discussion 的 §repro 段。
    25	
    26	## 二、被改变的结论：Route 1
    27	
    28	原文（撤回前的版本）：去掉泊车会话的记账**没有**双计，但它 admit 更多之后
    29	「墙钟慢 4.2 分钟、SLO 掉 6.0 pp，两项相抵」。这个「相抵」是用被污染的 τ₂（31.3 ms）算出来的。
    30	
    31	干净数据下：墙钟**快 25.4 分钟**（265.9 vs 291.3），SLO 掉 3.9 pp，
    32	而**两者相对各自位置的前沿距离几乎相同**（−1.24 vs −1.33，差 0.09 pp，
    33	同配置两次 run 的 Δ 散布是 0.5 pp）。
    34	
    35	所以修订后的结论更强、也更负面：**去掉记账不把控制点抬到包络之上，只把它沿前沿往下滑。**
    36	§V 里那一整段重写了，并且**明写这是修订**（原文「the two cancelled」改成
    37	「the cancellation was a property of the contamination, not of the mechanism」）。
    38	
    39	## 三、连带改动的清单
    40	
    41	因为这条臂同时出现在别处，改动是连锁的：
    42	
    43	1. **表 I**：`resident base` 行 → 265.9 / 2.41 / 85.9 / −1.33，去掉 `‡`。
    44	2. **表 I 脚注**：六条 `‡` 行 → 五条；四条受损 → 三条在重测、一条已重测干净并换数。
    45	3. **归因表**：`bres` 行 56.9/1.481/34.1「τ high, not matched‡」→ 56.2/1.481/29.5「unused mem.」。
    46	   **归因数 3 → 4**（三条 memory-unused、一条 step-cost、五条 τ-high、三条残差）；
    47	   τ-high 行 6 → 5，其中带旗 4 → 3。分类器只看在跑数与 τ₁，而新值让它在在跑数上
    48	   明显低于对照（1.481 vs cap5 的 1.842），所以归到「内存没用满」——这一格是数据决定的，
    49	   不是我们挑的。
    50	4. **§III 解构段**：`bres` 原本是「两个分量反向移动」的例证（空转降 17 分钟、忙时涨 21 分钟、
    51	   反而更慢）。干净数据下**两个分量同向下降**（空转 −9.5、忙时 −15.5、墙钟 −25.4），
    52	   这个例证不成立了。换成两条**未被损害**的臂：`default`（空转 −13.8、忙时 +11.1、
    53	   墙钟只快 2.7 分钟、SLO 89.8→3.0）与 `foyer_nogate`（空转 +10.1、忙时 −29.2、
    54	   墙钟 −19.0）。**结论方向不变，例证换了。**
    55	5. **图 2 图注**：空心柱 6 → 5；原来那段讲 `bres` 的话删掉，换成 `default` 的排位
    56	   （空转占比第 6、时长第 16）。
    57	6. **图 1 图注**：空心圈 6 → 5，并注明已重测的那条现在不带圈。
    58	7. **§III 未解释段**：三条受损 +「第四条已重测干净并入集合」；回归从 15 条变 16 条，
    59	   最大残差 1.4 → 1.5 ms（r 仍是 +0.92，没变）。
    60	8. **§II 阈值段**：超过 295 分钟的控制器变体 4 → 3 条（`bres` 回到 265.9）。
    61	9. **§VI 阈值敏感性表**：`bres` 行 −11.9/−7.8/−4.3 → −3.6/−1.4/+0.3。
    62	   **正文那段讲「松阈值下有一条越线」的论证不受影响**（越线的是 `horizon=1` 的 +1.5）。
    63	10. **§repro**：受损行 4 → 3 且写明 `bres` 已重测；「十五条 flag、六条进表」→ 十四条、五条。
    64	11. **§intro**：空转均值 20.6 → 20.9 s、>20 s 占比 69.6 → 69.9 %；
    65	    「bres 是例外」那句改成「§III 给两条分量反向移动的臂」。
    66	12. **§VI 重复对**：新增的 `realhr_foyer`/`realhr_foyer_r2` 对（0.06 % 墙钟、0.03 点兑现）
    67	    写进正文；两条 trace 上的重复对区间 1.4–6.1 % → 0.0–6.1 %（且明确是十组）。
    68	
    69	## 四、数据文件与生成器
    70	
    71	- `gen_eviction.py`：新增 `SUPERSEDED` 集合（`foyer_bres`，写明理由）。**这是必须的**：
    72	  判据自检（`_derive`）会把「被重跑替代」误报成「集合没说清」，而集合本来就必须
    73	  机械可重算。同时把 `RUNS` 从 13 条补到 24 条 —— 原来裸跑一次会静默把 eviction.csv
    74	  砍掉一半（文件里 24 行，默认列表 13 条）。
    75	- `gen_repeat_spread.py`：同样的 `SUPERSEDED` 排除。不加的话 `bres`/`bres_r2` 会归成
    76	  同一 policy 的一组、算出 **10.1 % 的「重复散布」**——那不是 run-to-run 噪声，
    77	  是污染 vs 干净的差，拿它当噪声上限是把这张表想量的东西量错。
    78	- `gen_gate_attrib.py`：**新增「只算跑完的 run」过滤**。不加的话正在跑的 `foyer_hz0_r2`
    79	  （275 个周期）会混进 all_rows 中位数。同一条护栏 `gen_projection.py` 早就有了。
    80	  实测把 all-rows 投影超预算中位从 27.4 拖到 27.3（含在跑的）／30.6（只算跑完的、且集合本身也变了）。
    81	- `gen_decomp.py` / `gen_hitrate.py`：裸跑的参数是**带 `pois200_` 前缀的名字**，
    82	  传短名会静默只写出 2 行（本次踩到，已重跑修正）。踩坑记录见下。
    83	- 逐 run 列表里 `foyer_bres` → `foyer_bres_r2` 的文件：`gen_runs.py`、`gen_table.py`、
    84	  `gen_occupancy.py`、`gen_arm_running.py`（两处）、`gen_attribution.py`、`gen_stall.py`、
    85	  `gen_gate_attrib.py`（两处）、`slo_sensitivity.py`（四处）、`idle_segments.py`、
    86	  `gen_idle_pooled.py`。
    87	
    88	## 五、本次踩到的坑（已记进 CLAUDE.md 的判断口径）
    89	
    90	1. **`gen_decomp.py` 传短名不报错，只写 2 行就成功退出。** 它要的是 `pois200_xxx`，
    91	   对不上的名字进 `bad` 列表打印一行、然后文件被正常写出来 —— 文件从 28 行变 2 行，
    92	   退出码是 0。**凡「按 run 列表生成」的脚本，都要断言产出行数等于列表长度。**
    93	   （本次靠 `check_paper_numbers.py` 的两项 MISS 事后发现。）
    94	2. **本地镜像里留着被替代的旧 run，会让「判据自检」永远失败。** 集合类判据需要
    95	   一个显式的「已作废」名单，否则每次重跑都要么卡住、要么把旧 run 算进去。
    96	3. **lab-only 的表会随「实验机上有什么」漂移**：`gen_gate_attrib` 的 all-rows 中位数
    97	   从 27.4 变 30.6，一部分是修了「含在跑的 run」这个 bug，一部分是集合本身长大了。
    98	   论文里引这类数**必须同时给出集合规模**（正文已改成「forty-two of them」）。

     1	# 发现：同机 ≥4 条车道会把步成本抬高 25~40%，四条控制器臂的数据作废
     2	
     3	**日期** 2026-09-24 · **脚本** `scripts/lab/gen_concurrency.py`（审计）、
     4	`scripts/lab/probe_concurrency.py`（时间分箱） · **数据** `paper/data/concurrency.csv`
     5	
     6	## 怎么发现的
     7	
     8	在做方向 B（把「host tier 决定准入控制有没有空间」做成主线）时，去查论文
     9	§III 自己标为「the largest open question」的那件事：HiCache 族的九条臂
    10	**重算量几乎恒定（8.6~9.1 M），但 τ₁ 却在 27.0~35.7 ms 之间散开**，论文原话是
    11	"Whatever moves τ there is not recomputation, and we do not know what it is."
    12	
    13	把八条 HiCache 臂的 τ₂（**逐请求 decode 间隔中位**，由 per-request 的
    14	`(total_duration − first_token)/(output_len − 1)` 算出，客户端实测、**不经过采样器**）
    15	按 R̄ 排开之后，出现了一个论文没注意到的分裂：
    16	
    17	| 组 | 臂 | τ₂ |
    18	|---|---|---|
    19	| 正常 | cap4_hc / cap3_hc / foyer_hc / foyer_hz1 / foyer_sf0_r2 | 23.8 ~ 25.7 |
    20	| **抬高** | **foyer_bres / foyer_hz0 / foyer_hz2 / foyer_breshz1** | **30.9 ~ 33.3** |
    21	
    22	bres（R̄ 1.481）与 cap3_hc（R̄ 1.509）、foyer_hz1（R̄ 1.524）**连占用率都匹配**
    23	（54.3% vs 55.5% / 54.1%），却慢 6.7 ms。四条高 τ₂ 的臂**恰好就是** 09-22 23:17–23:33
    24	同一分钟启动、彼此重叠约 300 分钟的那一批（详见下表），当时四台 SGLang server
    25	同机运行。
    26	
    27	## 判据与验证
    28	
    29	### 1. 不是时间局部的（排除"跑到一半有人挤进来"）
    30	
    31	把每条 run 的 τ₂ 沿时间分 6~16 箱取中位（`probe_concurrency.py`）：
    32	
    33	```
    34	foyer_hz2     33.0 33.1 30.5 31.1 27.9 24.6    全程高
    35	foyer_hz0     34.0 33.8 34.5 30.4 32.5 33.7    全程高
    36	foyer_breshz1 33.5 33.9 34.1 33.9 33.3 28.8    全程高
    37	foyer_hc      24.1 24.4 23.8 22.8 23.6 24.1    全程低
    38	cap4_hc       25.5 27.2 25.4 25.6 26.1 25.5    全程低
    39	```
    40	
    41	外部进程**中途挤进来**会表现为某几箱出现台阶。这里是平的，即**整个测量窗口都被
    42	同一个持续条件覆盖**——与"四条车道自始至终互相争用"一致。
    43	
    44	### 2. 对照实验 1：cap1 在车道数变化处的跳变
    45	
    46	`pois200_cap1`（20:13 起跑）独自跑了 5.5 小时，02:25 rl90/rl99 同时启动：
    47	
    48	```
    49	20:37 20:10 21:43 22:16 22:49 23:22 23:55 00:28 01:01 01:34 02:07 | 02:40 03:13 03:46 04:18 04:51
    50	20.5  20.3  21.2  20.6  20.4  20.6  20.4  20.4  20.6  20.5  20.7  | 22.4  27.5  20.9  25.4  24.0
    51	        └──────────── 1~2 条车道，恒在 20.3~20.7 ────────────┘        └── 3~4 条车道 ──┘
    52	```
    53	
    54	### 3. 对照实验 2：t85 与 rl99 在同一分钟同时回落
    55	
    56	两条**独立**的 run，τ₂ 在 07:09~07:23（rl90 结束前后）**同一时刻**从 ~30 掉到 ~22~25：
    57	
    58	```
    59	t85   30.0 25.8 30.9 25.1 31.0 29.5 30.3 | 22.3 23.7 22.6 22.0 22.4 23.4 23.5 24.3 23.4
    60	t90   29.6 25.1 30.2 27.1 30.5 27.3      | 21.4 23.6 21.9 22.2 23.4 23.1 23.5 23.4
    61	rl99  33.0 37.5 32.9 31.4 28.9 30.2 35.0 30.8 35.3 31.0 39.7 31.6 30.4 | 25.3 24.6
    62	                                        └── 07:09~07:23 同时回落 ──┘
    63	```
    64	
    65	两个独立的测量在同一个绝对时刻改变水平，只能是**共同的外部条件**，不可能是各自的
    66	工作负载或策略变化。
    67	
    68	### 4. 剂量：2~3 条不抬，4 条才抬
    69	
    70	| 并发 | 实例 | τ₂ |
    71	|---|---|---|
    72	| 2 条 | 绝大多数 run | 同族正常 |
    73	| 3 条 | `pairB_foyerhc`（277 min）、`cap3_fixed`（163 min）、`foyer_sf0_r2`、`foyer_hz1_r2`、`cap3_hc_r2` | 23.8 ~ 25.0，**均正常** |
    74	| **4 条** | `bres`/`hz0`/`hz2`/`breshz1`（各约 296 min） | **30.9 ~ 33.3** |
    75	| 4 条 | `_failed_4lane_*`（09-24 被 SIGTERM 的两条） | 31.4 / 32.1 |
    76	
    77	这一条正是 `CLAUDE.md` 里 2026-09-21 和 2026-09-24 两次踩到的同一个坑
    78	（"不要同时占满四张卡"，四车道会被别的租户的 reaper 杀掉）。当时的结论是
    79	"会被杀"，**这次发现的是更早的代价：即使没被杀，数据也已经不可比了**。
    80	
    81	## 审计结果
    82	
    83	`paper/data/concurrency.csv`（123 条 run）：
    84	
    85	- **CONTESTED（同时 ≥4 条持续 ≥60 分钟）12 条**
    86	- watch（≥3 条持续 ≥60 分钟）4 条
    87	- clean 107 条
    88	
    89	表 I 里受影响的臂正好 4 条，全部是控制器变体：
    90	
    91	| run | 峰值 | ≥4 条持续 | τ₂ |
    92	|---|---|---|---|
    93	| `foyer_breshz1` | 4 | 296 分钟 | 33.3 |
    94	| `foyer_hz0` | 4 | 296 分钟 | 33.1 |
    95	| `foyer_bres` | 4 | 296 分钟 | 31.3 |
    96	| `foyer_hz2` | 4 | 282 分钟 | 30.9 |
    97	
    98	其余 CONTESTED 的 8 条都不在表 I：rl90 / rl99 / t85 / t90（rl 与 t 的扫描）、
    99	var_foyer / var_cap3（变负载族）、以及 09-24 被 SIGTERM 的两条 `_failed_4lane_*`。
   100	
   101	`pois200_cap1` 只有 28.8 分钟处于 ≥4 条，记 watch；它的 τ₂ 20.6 是全场最低，
   102	未受影响。
   103	
   104	## 影响估计（重测之前，只作量级参考，不进正文）
   105	
   106	τ 若按同族干净水平折算，这四条的忙时会缩短约 22%，墙钟大致：
   107	
   108	```
   109	foyer_bres    295.5 → ~247 分    对前沿在该点的插值 91.6  →  Δ 由 −7.8 变到约 −0.6
   110	```
   111	
   112	**如果这个量级成立，表 I 的判决会变**——这四条的 Δ 可能从"明显在线下"变成
   113	"贴着线"。所以必须重测，不能靠折算。
   114	
   115	## 已采取的动作
   116	
   117	1. **重跑这四条**（`lane_foyer_r2_batch1.sh` / `lane_foyer_r2_batch2.sh`），
   118	   新 run 名加 `_r2` 后缀，严格 2 条车道。旧目录原样保留，不覆盖不删除。
   119	2. 重跑用 `wait_and_launch.sh` 排队：等前一条车道的**最后一个 run 写出 summary**
   120	   （不是"显存空"——run_matrix 在一条车道的两条 run 之间会卸载 server，
   121	   那一刻显存是空的但车道没结束），启动前再数一次自己的 server 数。
   122	3. 审计脚本进仓，`paper/data/concurrency.csv` 由它生成。
   123	
   124	## 对论文的意义（要如实写）
   125	
   126	- **表 I 的这四行在重测前不可信**。重测完用新 run 替换，并在可复现性一节
   127	  说明为什么换、怎么发现的。
   128	- 这是一条**方法论发现**，本身值得写：内存受限 serving 的测量对同机并发
   129	  **高度敏感**，而敏感性只体现在 `τ₂` 这种**逐请求的客户端量**上——引擎侧
   130	  `num_running_reqs`、占用率、驱逐量、重算量**全都看不出异常**（这四条臂在这四列上
   131	  与干净 run 不可区分）。如果只盯着引擎指标，这批数据会被当成好的。
   132	- 与 §III 那条"最大未解问题"的关系：论文原来写"HiCache 族重算恒定而 τ 散开，
   133	  我们不知道为什么"。**其中一部分现在有答案了——那四条臂的 τ 不是被什么都西推动的，
   134	  是被同机争用推动的**。剩下五条（hc/hz1/hz2 之外的）重算恒定而 τ 在 27.0~29.2 之间
   135	  小范围移动，这是重测之后要重新面对的问题，范围比原来小得多。

     1	# 发现：复用兑现率跨 trace **不能**用一个占用阈值概括
     2	
     3	**日期** 2026-09-24 · **数据** `paper/data/reuse.csv` + realhr 四条 run 的原始日志
     4	**检验** 方向 B 的主线主张「容量决定复用兑现率」能不能迁移到第二条 trace
     5	
     6	## 怎么发现的
     7	
     8	做方向 B 时去查：realhr（第二条 trace，device-only，与我们主 trace 同池子 101,432）
     9	的静态点能不能复现主 trace 的兑现率曲线。四条 run 的日志齐全，不用等新实验。
    10	
    11	用与 `gen_reuse.py` 完全相同的定义算（上界从 trace 的 `prefix_len` 求和独立得到，
    12	runner 的 `summary.json` 里也有同一个数，两处一致）：
    13	
    14	| run | R̄_busy | 占用 p50 | 计划上界 | 实测复用 | **兑现率** |
    15	|---|---|---|---|---|---|
    16	| `realhr_cap2` | 1.076 | **15.8%** | 86.27% | 64.51% | **74.8%** |
    17	| `realhr_cap3` | 1.160 | 18.4% | 86.27% | 62.24% | **72.2%** |
    18	| `realhr_cap4` | 1.287 | 19.8% | 86.27% | 58.23% | **67.5%** |
    19	| `realhr_foyer` | 1.222 | 19.4% | 86.27% | 59.84% | **69.4%** |
    20	
    21	对照主 trace 的 device-only 五条：
    22	
    23	| run | R̄_busy | 占用 p50 | 计划上界 | 兑现率 |
    24	|---|---|---|---|---|
    25	| `cap1` | 1.000 | **22.4%** | 75.51% | **92.6%** |
    26	| `cap2` | 1.271 | 41.6% | 75.51% | 89.6% |
    27	| `cap3_fixed` | 1.534 | 59.0% | 75.51% | 78.9% |
    28	| `cap4` | 1.650 | 66.4% | 75.51% | 60.6% |
    29	| `cap5` | 1.842 | 74.3% | 75.51% | 36.3% |
    30	
    31	## 结论一（支持）：两条 trace 上都出现兑现率随并发下降
    32	
    33	这是好的：**「加并发 → 兑现率掉 → 重算补上」不是主 trace 的孤例。**
    34	realhr 从 74.8% 掉到 67.5%（R̄ 1.076→1.287），direction 一致。
    35	
    36	按 R̄ 计的斜率，realhr 甚至更陡：
    37	- 主 trace：−3.0pp / ΔR̄ 0.27 = **−11.1 pp/单位**
    38	- realhr：−7.3pp / ΔR̄ 0.21 = **−34.5 pp/单位**
    39	
    40	## 结论二（**不支持**）：兑现率不能用占用率概括，跨 trace 更不行
    41	
    42	主 trace 在占用 **22.4%** 时兑现 92.6%；realhr 在占用 **15.8%**（更低！）时只兑现
    43	74.8%。**占用更低而兑现更差，方向是反的。**
    44	
    45	所以「池子占到 X% 就开始塌」这种说法**不成立**，跨 trace 尤其不成立。可能的原因
    46	（都**没有**测过，不许当结论写）：
    47	- 占用率是**瞬时**量，而复用取决于**历史**：realhr 的 117 个会话在两小时内轮流，
    48	  每个会话的上一轮前缀要活到下一轮才谈得上复用，而这一段的淘汰压力不体现在
    49	  瞬时占用上；
    50	- 两条 trace 的会话大小、轮次间隔、到达统计都不同（realhr 每步 prompt 18.6K，
    51	  主 trace 28.6K）。
    52	
    53	## 对论文的影响
    54	
    55	1. **§III 那段「梯队决定兑现」在【同一配置内 + host tier 对照】上仍然成立**，
    56	   那个对照是匹配占用的（cap3_fixed 55.8% vs cap3_hc 55.5%），不受本条影响。
    57	2. **但不能写成「容量/占用决定兑现率」这种带可迁移阈值的说法。** 摘要与引言里
    58	   「the tier decides how much of the bound survives」这个措辞仍然安全（它讲的是
    59	   梯队这个**变量**起作用），但**任何暗示「占用率是那个自变量」的句子都要去掉**。
    60	3. 这与论文既有的立场一致：§IV 已经承认弹性判据是「工作负载 + 配置的诊断，
    61	   不是预测器」。现在复用兑现率有同样的性质，**应该一起说**，而不是假装指数
    62	   干净。
    63	4. **这让半池实验变得更重要**：同一张卡、同一条 trace、同一个模型，只动池子大小，
    64	   是目前唯一能把「容量」这一个变量单独拎出来的实验。跨 trace 做不到。
    65	
    66	## 待办
    67	
    68	- 半池实验（`lane_pool50.sh`，已排队）落地后，用同一套定义算兑现率，
    69	  看它是否随池子大小单调移动。**这是唯一能定因果的那个实验。**
    70	- realhr 的 cap5/cap6/cap8（正在跑）会告诉我们兑现率在更高 R̄ 处掉到哪里 ——
    71	  如果它在占用仍低的情况下继续掉，那「占用不是自变量」这条就被进一步坐实。

     1	# 主张 → 证据 审计（2026-09-23）
     2	
     3	> 目的：写正文之前，把活下来的每一条主张拉出来逐条查。
     4	> 判据是四样，**缺任何一样就降级或删掉**：
     5	>
     6	> 1. **实测数字**（不是推论）
     7	> 2. **对照**（不是孤立观测）
     8	> 3. **反例检查**（我们自己试过推翻它没有）
     9	> 4. **可复现路径**（命令 / 脚本 / run 名）
    10	>
    11	> 这个项目的**六条**撤回（步成本的分母、`tool_wait` 0.1s→16.85s、oracle 单调性/增长敏感度、
    12	> 重复计费、空转率单调性、以及「驱逐→重算」那次错误的反驳）都是因为缺了第 3 条。
    13	> 六条的清单与论文 §讨论 逐条对应；此前这里写「五条」，漏了「步成本的分母」那条
    14	> —— 而它恰好是唯一改过头条数字的一次（2026-09-24 对齐）。
    15	
    16	---
    17

   285	made things worse. (Two denominators appear here and we name them: the 75.5\%
   286	is the trace's bound as a share of the prompt, and 92.5\% is the best observed
   287	reuse as a share of \emph{that bound}---\texttt{cap1}, the run that realizes
   288	it, reuses 69.9\% of its prompt. \texttt{reuse.csv} carries \texttt{reuse\_frac} and
   289	\texttt{realized\_frac} side by side so the two cannot be confused.)
   290	
   291	This is the same collapse \S\ref{sec:related} reports under a different
   292	normalization. That section quotes the round-1-and-later prefix hit rate---%
   293	steps restricted to rounds $\ge 1$, unweighted mean over steps---which falls
   294	from 80.8\% to 32.8\% across the device-only caps and holds at 80.7\% to
   295	78.7\% with a host tier. The two conventions differ in two ways, not one:
   296	which steps are in the set (round $\ge 1$ against all rounds) and how steps
   297	are combined (unweighted mean against token-weighted sum). We use the
   298	token-weighted all-steps convention here because reuse and recomputation must
   299	add to the prompt, and we name the other whenever we quote it. Both are
   300	\emph{realized} quantities that vary with the policy; neither is the trace's
   301	planned rate, which is fixed at 75.5\% and appears in this section only as
   302	the bound.
   303	
   304	Device occupancy does not explain the split. \texttt{cap3\_fixed} and
   305	\texttt{cap3\_hc} run at nearly the same mean device occupancy (55.8\% and
   306	55.5\%) and nearly the same running count (1.53 and 1.51), and they realize
   307	79.0\% and 91.3\% of the bound respectively. What differs is not how full the
   308	device pool is but what happens when it overflows: with no host tier an
   309	evicted prefix is gone and must be recomputed; with one it is demoted and read
   310	back. The pool is the binding resource, the tier sets the pool, and the tier
   311	therefore sets whether added concurrency is paid for in recomputation. The
   312	caveat is that this pair is two runs on two different days and two different
   313	GPUs, not a paired experiment; \S\ref{sec:repro} states what we did to rule
   314	out co-tenancy. A capacity-controlled version---same card, same trace, only
   315	the pool size changed---is queued behind the re-runs and we report it in
   316	\S\ref{sec:results} when it lands; we do not use it here.
   317	
   318	\paragraph{The second trace agrees on direction and not on level.}
   319	We ran the same accounting on a second workload, reported in full in
   320	\S\ref{sec:xtrace}---117 real Claude agent sessions, 390 rounds, the same pool
   321	and the same engine---and it reproduces the direction: its realization of \emph{its own} bound falls from 74.8\% to 57.5\%
   322	as the running count rises from 1.08 to 1.50, and over that span the whole-range
   323	per-unit slope is $-40.9$ points, against the device-only family's $-66.8$ over
   324	$\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ 1.00--1.84 (\texttt{slope.csv}). What it does not reproduce is the level, and this is
   325	worth stating because it bounds what we can claim. The lightest cap on that
   326	trace (\texttt{realhr\_cap2}) runs at a \emph{lower} median device occupancy
   327	than the lightest on this one (15.8\% against 22.4\%, \texttt{cap1}) and
   328	realizes \emph{less} (74.8\% against 92.5\%), so realization is not a function
   329	of occupancy alone---and a
   330	statement of the form ``the pool collapses above $x$\% occupancy'' is wrong on
   331	our own data. Occupancy is instantaneous while reuse depends on history; a
   332	session's prefix must survive from one round to the next, and how much
   333	pressure that puts on the pool is not visible in what the pool holds at an
   334	instant. We do not have the measurement that would separate those, which is
   335	why the controlled experiment we report in \S\ref{sec:results} varies the
   336	pool alone, on one card and one trace, rather than leaning on the cross-trace
   337	comparison. The claim this section supports is the narrow one: within a
   338	configuration, the host tier preserves realization at a matched running count
   339	and a matched occupancy, and without it realization falls.
   340	
   341	That gives the criterion of \S\ref{sec:criterion} a mechanism rather than a
   342	correlation, and it suggests a prediction we are testing:
   343	if the tier acts by multiplying usable capacity (the host pool is twice the
   344	device pool, so three times in total), then shrinking the device pool alone
   345	should move the collapse point even with no host tier involved. We do not
   346	treat the arithmetic as a result---it assumes KV demand grows linearly in the
   347	running count, which heterogeneous sessions need not satisfy---and the
   348	queued experiment measures the collapse point directly rather than predicting
   349	it, and \S\ref{sec:results} reports it once it has run.
   350	
   351	\paragraph{What is left unexplained.} Less than an earlier draft of this
   352	paragraph claimed. Across the nineteen host-tier runs recomputation spans
   353	8.6--12.8\,M tokens and realization 72.8--92.5\%, and $\tau_1$ spans
   354	27.0--36.2\,ms. Three of those runs
   355	(\texttt{F:hz2} 33.6, \texttt{F:hz0} 35.7,
   356	\texttt{F:breshz1} 36.2\,ms) were
   357	measured while three other engine instances shared the host, which inflates
   358	per-step cost by 25--40\% and leaves the volume columns untouched
   359	(\S\ref{sec:repro}); they are being re-measured and we do not use their
   360	timing. A fourth was re-measured clean and is in the set above with its new
   361	value. Across the remaining sixteen the $\tau_1$ spread does not sit
   362	unexplained at all: it tracks their recomputation closely (a straight line
   363	through the sixteen leaves every run within $1.5$\,ms, $r = +0.92$), which is
   364	the mechanism of this section holding inside the host tier rather than a
   365	residual. We report the association and not the line: the coefficients are
   366	fitted to one configuration family and we have no derivation for them. We note that the negative
   367	result does not rest on it either way: the cancellation is measured directly,
   368	not inferred from the mechanism.
   369	
   370	One caution about the strength of the account above, since the arithmetic
   371	invites overreading. $\text{reuse} + \text{recomputed} = \text{prompt}$ is a
   372	definition, not a finding, so ``the shortfall reappears as recomputation''
   373	follows from how we partitioned the prompt and cannot by itself establish the
   374	mechanism. What is independent of the partition is the pair of engine
   375	counters: evicted tokens and uncached prefill tokens are separate SGLang
   376	counters, and on the device-only runs they track within 0.6--0.7\,M on all
   377	five---which is the observation that licenses reading eviction as the cause
   378	of recomputation. With a host tier the two come apart by design, and the
   379	1.3--8.6\,M gap between them is an upper bound on what the host absorbed out
   380	of the evicted pool: those tokens were evicted and not recomputed, which is
   381	consistent with the host having served them and equally consistent with their
   382	never having been asked for again. We can bound it, not identify it
   383	(\texttt{eviction.csv}); the host serves 9.5--19.3\,M in total, so most of what
   384	it serves are prefixes that were still resident on the device when the
   385	session came back for them.

     1	\section{The Same Measurements on a Second Workload}
     2	\label{sec:xtrace}
     3	
     4	Everything so far is one trace family, and a negative result drawn from one
     5	family invites the objection that the family is the finding. We ran the same
     6	accounting, a static sweep from a cap of two to a cap of eight, and the same
     7	controller on a second replay:
     8	117 real Claude agent sessions, 390 rounds, the same model, the same engine and
     9	the same 101{,}432-token pool. One difference has to be stated up front,
    10	because it bears on the paper's central variable: \emph{this trace was run
    11	entirely without a host tier}. Its static points, its envelope and its
    12	controller are all device-only. That makes the comparison internally fair---both
    13	sides of the front are the same configuration---but it means this workload
    14	tests the negative result and not the tier mechanism, and it leaves an
    15	alternative account of the controller's failure standing: it failed here on the
    16	configuration where \S\ref{sec:model} says added concurrency is expensive. The two workloads differ in ways that matter for
    17	this paper, which is why we chose it. Its sessions are smaller (median prompt
    18	18{,}809 tokens against 23{,}187) and its tool waits are far longer at the
    19	median: over round transitions---same session,
    20	adjacent rounds, each session's last round excluded because it has no
    21	successor---the gap is 20.2\,s against 0.34\,s at the median---about 60$\times$---and
    22	39.9\,s against 15.8\,s at the mean, which is 2.5$\times$
    23	(\texttt{toolwait.csv} carries both traces on this definition). The 29.1\,s
    24	that appears in \texttt{elasticity\_x.csv} is the mean over all 390 rows
    25	including each session's last round, and is not comparable with the main
    26	trace's 15.8\,s; we use the transition definition throughout §\ref{sec:xtrace}. So the engine is idle far more of the
    27	time, and the running counts are lower.
    28	
    29	\subsection{What transfers, and what does not}
    30	
    31	\begin{figure}[tb]
    32	  \centering
    33	  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig8_transfer.pdf}
    34	  \caption{Realization against the running count on both traces, each
    35	  normalized to its own trace's reuse bound. Device-only, the main trace falls
    36	  from 92.5\% to 36.4\% and the second from 74.8\% to 57.5\%; with a host
    37	  tier the main trace's runs stay at 72.8--92.5\%. The second trace is lower
    38	  throughout---interpolating the main trace's line at the second trace's
    39	  running counts gives a 17-point gap at the lowest and 23 at the
    40	  highest---and it declines more gently, which is what ``the direction
    41	  transfers and the level does not'' looks like on this axis.}
    42	  \label{fig:transfer}
    43	\end{figure}
    44	
    45	\paragraph{The reuse accounting transfers in direction, not in level.} The
    46	second trace plans a higher reuse bound than the first---86.3\% of its prompt
    47	tokens against 75.5\%---and realizes less of it, at a lower device occupancy
    48	(Fig.~\ref{fig:transfer}).
    49	Realization falls from 74.8\% to 57.5\% as the running count rises from 1.08 to
    50	1.50, so the direction of \S\ref{sec:model} survives. What does \emph{not}
    51	survive is the sharpness. On the main trace the decline is a threshold: the
    52	per-unit slope is $-10.4$, $-40.6$, then $-157.1$ and $-127.6$ points per unit
    53	as the running count passes 1.53, which is the cliff \S\ref{sec:criterion}
    54	measures. Here the slope stays gentler through the same region---$-31.2$,
    55	$-36.8$, $-28.4$, $-93.0$ and $-67.6$---and the two steep numbers at the end
    56	sit on spans of 0.017 and 0.074 of running count, where a difference between
    57	two runs starts to read as a trend. Taken as a whole the sweep loses 74.8--57.5\%
    58	of this trace's bound between $\overline{R}$ 1.08 and 1.50 at $-40.9$ points
    59	per unit. We do compare the two
    60	traces' overall slopes, which is the one slope comparison the data supports
    61	because both are taken over their full measured ranges
    62	(\texttt{slope.csv}): the device-only family runs from 92.5\% to 36.4\% over
    63	$\overline{R}$ 1.000--1.842, a whole-range slope of $-66.8$ points per unit,
    64	and this trace from 74.8\% to 57.5\% over 1.08--1.50, a slope of $-40.9$.
    65	So per unit of running count, the main trace loses its bound about one and a
    66	half times as fast over its range ($-66.8$ against $-40.9$)---but the
    67	comparison is between ranges of different length and
    68	different absolute level, and we do not lean on it.
    69	What this trace does establish is that the \emph{level} does not transfer, and
    70	we treat that as a bound on what we can
    71	claim: it realizes \emph{less} at 15.8\% median device occupancy than the
    72	main trace does at 22.4\%---the two numbers are the lightest cap on each trace
    73	(\texttt{realhr\_cap2} realizing 74.8\% and \texttt{cap1} realizing 92.5\%), both
    74	medians of \texttt{sglang:token\_usage} with means of 13.9\% and 25.2\%
    75	(\texttt{reuse\_x.csv}, \texttt{occupancy.csv})---so realization is not a
    76	function of occupancy alone and a
    77	rule of the form ``the pool collapses above $x$\% occupancy'' is false on our
    78	own data. Occupancy is instantaneous; reuse depends on whether a prefix
    79	survives from one round to the next, which is a history the instantaneous
    80	number does not carry. We do not measure that history here, and we say so
    81	rather than fitting the two traces to a common curve.
    82	
    83	\paragraph{The elasticity criterion transfers in sign, not in place.} On both
    84	traces the criterion plays the same role, and it is the modest one
    85	\S\ref{sec:criterion} settles on: it \emph{rules out} the workload-side
    86	story. The controller on this trace runs at a busy-time mean of 1.223, which
    87	falls inside the span its own static caps cover (1.08 to 1.50)---more
    88	precisely inside the cap3$\to$cap4 segment, where
    89	$\varepsilon(\tau_1) = -0.28$ over $\overline{R}$ 1.16--1.29, so its failure is
    90	again not explained by the criterion.
    91	What the completed sweep adds is that this trace does approach the criterion's
    92	\emph{expensive} branch, later and more gently than the main trace: above
    93	$\overline{R} \approx 1.29$ the model-consistent elasticity turns positive and
    94	touches one in the first of the three segments there ($+1.0$, then $+0.18$ and
    95	$+0.87$ as the sweep reaches 1.50), while the main trace crosses one at 1.53
    96	with $+1.15$ and stays above it. So the direction is not a property of one
    97	family; the location is. We report the point estimates and not a threshold, because a $\pm10\%$
    98	perturbation of $\tau_1$---inside the range our repeat pairs show---carries
    99	each of those three segments' bounds across zero \emph{and} across one. The per-request
   100	measure is no steadier ($+0.45$, $-1.25$, $+0.73$). And the controller never
   101	operates where the branch turns expensive: 1.22, below 1.29 and below the main
   102	trace's 1.53 alike.
   103	
   104	We also record what the noise does, because it is the same weakness the whole
   105	paper has. Recomputing each segment's $\varepsilon$ with $\tau_1$ perturbed
   106	by $\pm10$\%---against the 0.3--1.1\% our clean repeat pairs differ by in
   107	$\tau_1$ (\texttt{decomp.csv}; one anomalous pair reaches 42.5\%), so the
   108	perturbation is generous rather than conservative---gives bands of
   109	$[-5.20, +0.41]$ for cap2$\to$cap3,
   110	$[-2.17, +1.61]$ for cap3$\to$cap4, $[-1.25, +3.26]$ for cap4$\to$cap5,
   111	$[-14.07, +14.43]$ for cap5$\to$cap6 and $[-3.33, +5.07]$ for
   112	cap6$\to$cap8. Every one of them crosses zero, and the three at the end cross
   113	one as well. So at that perturbation no segment's sign is resolvable, and the
   114	turn the paragraph above describes is a property of the point estimates rather
   115	than a boundary we have located---which is a bound on that paragraph, not a
   116	reprieve from it.
   117	
   118	\paragraph{The controller's verdict transfers, weakly.} \sys{} finishes the
   119	second trace in 75.6 minutes, against 66.2 for the fastest static run on it
   120	(the cap of eight), so \sys{} is 14.3\% slower (14.26\% at full precision).
   121	We do not report that as a loss. The repeat pairs we have vary by 1.7--4.7\%
   122	in wall clock (\texttt{replicates.csv}), and this trace gives us two of its
   123	own: the cap of four repeats to within 1.7\%, and \sys{} itself to within
   124	0.06\% in wall clock and 0.03 points of realization. The gap is more than
   125	eight times the wider of those, so it is not repeat noise on either side of
   126	it---but two pairs cannot bound the variability of the \emph{other}
   127	configurations in the comparison, and run-to-run spread on this machine
   128	reaches 22.0\% across the
   129	other configurations and tiers we have measured (\texttt{repeat\_spread.csv};
   130	0.0--6.1\% across the ten pairs those two traces give us, the spread between
   131	the pairs themselves being the reason we report a range and not a number).
   132	So the honest reading is the one we have used throughout: at the threshold
   133	this paper uses, it did not win on
   134	either axis.
   135	Its signed distance from the second trace's
   136	static envelope is $+0.40$\,pp at $10$\,s on
   137	the SLO axis---inside the $\pm1.0$\,pp tie band, which is defined on that
   138	axis---and $-0.14$\,pp on the cache-hit axis, against the 14.9 points that
   139	separate this trace's own six static caps on that axis. That axis is
   140	threshold-dependent here, and in the opposite direction from the main trace's:
   141	at $5$\,s this trace's caps separate much more (the cap of eight scores
   142	$78.2$\% against the cap of two's $99.0$\%) and the controller sits
   143	$+1.87$\,pp above the envelope---outside the band, a win on that axis---whereas
   144	on the main trace no controller reaches the front at $5$\,s and one crosses at
   145	$20$\,s (\S\ref{sec:discussion}). We report both readings rather than the one
   146	that gives a single answer. (The envelope and the
   147	elasticity below are both built from all six static caps the sweep now has;
   148	the three caps the sweep added after the first version of this comparison---the
   149	caps of five, six and eight---are the reason the last three segments exist
   150	at all.)
   151	The SLO number is nearly uninformative, and we flag that rather than let it
   152	stand as a measurement. At the three caps the envelope was originally built
   153	from, every point on this trace scored 99.5--100.0\%, so the axis could not
   154	discriminate at all. The three the sweep has since added do separate---the cap
   155	of eight scores 93.8\% against the cap of two's 100.0\%---so the axis is not
   156	flat after all, but its whole spread over six static caps is 6.2 points against
   157	a $\pm1.0$\,pp tie band, and \sys{} sits at 100.0\% on it.
   158	
   159	\subsection{What this section does and does not buy}
   160	
   161	It buys a negative result that is not a property of one trace family: on a
   162	second workload, with a different session-size distribution and a
   163	tool-wait distribution about 60$\times$ longer at the median, the same controller
   164	still does not beat the same kind of static cap, and the reuse degradation
   165	still appears. It costs us the transportability of the threshold. On the main trace the
   166	criterion narrowed the search by excluding the workload-side explanation and
   167	put the turn of the point estimates at $\overline{R} \approx 1.53$; on this
   168	trace the same measurement turns positive above 1.29. What we cannot do is
   169	\emph{locate} either turn---the $\pm10\%$ perturbation puts every segment's
   170	bounds across zero on both traces---which is the caveat
   171	\S\ref{sec:criterion} already states for the main trace, now with a second
   172	trace behind it. The controller is below both turns (1.22), so the criterion
   173	does not explain its failure on either trace. We
   174	report both, and we note that the second workload is closer to the regime
   175	where adaptive control \emph{should} pay---lower concurrency, much longer idle
   176	gaps---and the controller still did not win there.

   478	around a capacity cliff looks like.
   479	
   480	We report two things honestly about this comparison. First, this is our
   481	reimplementation of a published control law, not the authors' system, and a
   482	reimplementation of any controller can underperform its paper. We built it to
   483	the published law, gave it the same permit interface and the same 2\,s cycle
   484	as \sys{}, and did not tune it beyond the two documented settings; but the
   485	caveat stands. Second, both controllers stall. At its paper parameters this one pinned its
   486	window at one session, admitted nothing, and left the engine idle for over an
   487	hour. Our own controller does the same thing more briefly: in 4 of the 11
   488	\sys{} runs a scan finds windows in which the engine was idle, 85 or more
   489	sessions were waiting, the SLO valve was open and the controller issued no
   490	permit at all. Three of those runs have a single such window each---170.5, 173.2
   491	and 191.5\,s, 0.9--1.1\% of the run's wall clock---and the arm that disables
   492	every throttle has nine of them, together 12\% of its wall clock (\texttt{stall.csv} states
   493	the criterion; an earlier draft quoted 150--175\,s and 0.9\% for all four,
   494	which no version of the criterion reproduces). The remaining runs, including
   495	the best one, show no such window. Nothing is logged
   496	as an error and the run recovers on its own.
   497	
   498	We traced one of those windows to a
   499	code path that explains it: single-flight admission considered only the head of
   500	the FIFO queue, and when the head was a session too large to fit the remaining
   501	budget it was never admitted, never left the queue, and blocked every smaller
   502	session behind it. That is the code every run in this paper executed. The fix
   503	in our repository---scan the queue, still admit at most one per cycle---is
   504	\emph{not} in the artifact that produced these results, so the numbers here
   505	predate it and we do not claim them for it. We are also explicit that this is
   506	\emph{a} path, not a proven cause:
   507	the audit is a log signature, and the same signature can be produced by other
   508	closed gates. What we can say is that the signature is present in a third of
   509	our \sys{} runs and absent from all of our static caps, which have no
   510	admission decision to get stuck in.
   511	
   512	That is the honest version of a finding we first reported too strongly. An
   513	intermediate draft of this paper called it a deadlock and said the
   514	configuration that produced our best result was the one that deadlocked on its
   515	repeat. Neither holds: the windows are minutes, not hours, and they clear on their
   516	own, and the configuration that produced our best result never showed one. Nor
   517	do they move any verdict we report---0.9--1.1\% of the wall clock in each of
   518	the three runs that has a single one, and in the all-throttles-off arm 12\% of
   519	a wall clock that is already the slowest in the table. We keep them because the mechanism is real and its size
   520	is the point: on a pool that holds four median sessions, a controller's failure
   521	mode is a silent stall, not graceful degradation, and it took a repeat run to
   522	see it.
   523	
   524	\subsection{Route 4: execution granularity}
   525	\label{sec:results:gate}
   526	
   527	The first three routes all assume that what a controller can set is \emph{how
   528	many sessions are resident}. A static cap sets the same quantity, so it is
   529	unsurprising that they land in the same place. The fourth route attacks the
   530	assumption instead: because a large fraction of live sessions are parked at
   531	any instant, a cap on \emph{resident} sessions leaves the engine idle whenever
   532	all residents happen to be parked. Capping the \emph{running-request} count
   533	instead should keep the engine busy while leaving the resident count high.
   534	
   535	The permit design supports this directly: a paused session finishes its
   536	current round and blocks before its next one, and resumes when the controller
   537	re-admits it. We implemented it as a gate on the engine's own
   538	\texttt{num\_running\_reqs} and swept the gate over $\{2,4\}$. Only the
   539	gate-of-four arm ran to 998 of the trace's 999 steps; the gate-of-two arm
   540	reached 770 and is not used, so what we report below is a single comparison
   541	against the no-gate arm rather than a sweep.
   542	
   543	It does not work, and the measurement says why. Against the no-gate arm, a
   544	gate of four moved mean running requests from 1.33 to 1.30 and mean live
   545	sessions from 3.25 to 3.03: it tightened both, by 2\% and 7\%, rather than
   546	pinning the one it aims at. That is what we
   547	observe, and we state it as the observation it is rather than deriving a bound
   548	we cannot support. A live session spends most of its time parked in a tool wait
   549	(the ratio of mean running requests to mean live sessions is 0.33 to 0.64
   550	across the twenty-three arms, \texttt{occupancy.csv}'s \texttt{running\_mean}
   551	over \texttt{runs.csv}'s \texttt{conc}), so pausing live sessions removes
   552	capacity that was not running in the first place---but that ratio is an average over a run, not a
   553	bound on any instant, and the running count can in principle reach the live
   554	count. Both repairs fail: without a
   555	constraint on admission, a paused session is re-admitted on the next cycle and
   556	blocks for at most one 2\,s cycle; with one, the policy is a session cap again.
   557	The obstacle is granularity---\texttt{paused} takes effect at round
   558	boundaries, while pinning an instantaneous count requires control the
   559	permit mechanism does not have.
   560	
   561	The measured outcome matches: the gate moved running concurrency from 1.33 to
   562	1.30 and live sessions from 3.25 to 3.03. It tightened rather than pinned, and
   563	a tightening is another cap.
   564	
   565	\begin{figure}[tb]
   566	  \centering
   567	  \includegraphics[width=\columnwidth]{figs/fig6_horizon.pdf}
   568	  \caption{The growth reserve is not a free parameter. Removing it costs
   569	  12.6\,pp of SLO; two rounds is 15 minutes slower than three. The optimum at
   570	  one round comes closest to the static front, 0.9\,pp below it---and is the
   571	  one point here that does not reproduce on a repeat (it lands 5.6\,pp
   572	  below).
   573	  The other three settings were each run once, so the shape is better
   574	  supported than any single point on it.}
   575	  \label{fig:horizon}

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""同一配置的重复 run 之间的墙钟散布 —— paper/data/repeat_spread.csv。
     3	
     4	论文 §VI 说「我们报告的 14.3% 差距不当作输，因为这台机器上跨配置与档位的
     5	run-to-run 墙钟散布可达 22.4%」。这个 22.4% 原先是 `xtrace_validation.md` 里
     6	手工算的（不在任何数据文件里），属于「数字追不到源头」那一类。
     7	
     8	判据（写死）：把 results 目录下**同 policy + 同 trace**、落地 ≥2 条的 run 归为一组，
     9	按墙钟取 (max-min)/mean。**必须在实验机上跑**：它是对 run 集合计数，本地镜像不全会
    10	少组、把上限压低（这正是 2026-09-24 栽过的那类）。
    11	
    12	用法（实验机）: python3 gen_repeat_spread.py <results_dir> <out.csv>
    13	"""
    14	import csv, glob, json, os, statistics as sys_st, sys
    15	
    16	MIN_RUNS = 40   # 最少要看到这么多带 metadata 的 run，否则拒跑（镜像不全的信号）
    17	
    18	# 【争用作废】的旧测量：它们与自己的 _r2 是同一 policy，会天然归成一组，
    19	# 而那一组算出来的不是 run-to-run 噪声，是【污染 vs 干净】的差 —— 拿它当
    20	# 「重复散布」会把上限抬高、把这张表想量的东西量错。判据与 gen_eviction.py
    21	# 的 SUPERSEDED 同一份名单，理由同源（docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md）。
    22	# 该 pair 的差值本身照旧引用（§discussion 用它证明体积列看不出污染），
    23	# 只是不进这张表。
    24	SUPERSEDED = {"pois200_foyer_bres"}
    25	
    26	
    27	def one(d):
    28	    """分组键必须把【配置与代码代】也带上。
    29	
    30	    第一版只用 (policy, trace)，于是把 `static=4` 的 device-only 与 +HiCache 两条、
    31	    以及不同代码代的 run 归成一组，算出 58.2% 的「散布」——那不是 run-to-run 噪声，
    32	    是配置与代码代的差异。判据要的是【同一配置、同一代码代】的重复。
    33	    """
    34	    m = json.load(open(d))
    35	    code = m.get("code") or {}
    36	    # 代码代指纹：controller_budget3 + session_runner + run_matrix 的哈希拼起来
    37	    # 用【整个 code 块】当指纹：不同代码代的键集合本身就不一样（老 run 没有
    38	    # controller_foyer.py 等），写死键名会得到半空指纹，把本该同代的 run 拆散。
    39	    fp = "|".join("%s=%s" % (k, code[k]) for k in sorted(code))
    40	    hc = bool((m.get("build") or {}).get("hicache_enabled"))
    41	    # 分组只用 (policy, trace, HiCache)；代码指纹不定，只作为「这一组是否同代码代」的标注。
    42	    return (m.get("policy"), os.path.basename(m.get("trace") or ""), hc), fp, m.get("wall_s")
    43	
    44	
    45	def main():
    46	    root, out_p = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
    47	    n_dirs = len(glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "*", "metadata.json")))
    48	    assert n_dirs >= MIN_RUNS, (
    49	        "只有 %d 个 run 目录（需要 >=%d）—— 这多半是部分镜像，本脚本要在实验机上跑。"
    50	        % (n_dirs, MIN_RUNS))
    51	    groups = {}
    52	    skipped, superseded = [], []
    53	    for d in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "*", "metadata.json"))):
    54	        run = os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(d))
    55	        if run.startswith("_"):
    56	            continue
    57	        if run in SUPERSEDED:
    58	            superseded.append(run)
    59	            continue
    60	        # 只算【跑完的】run：中止/超时的 run（如 _timedout_591steps）进组会把
    61	        # 「散布」算成 58%（第一版就是这样），那不是噪声是事故。
    62	        if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(d), "summary.json")):
    63	            skipped.append(run)
    64	            continue
    65	        try:
    66	            key, fp, wall = one(d)
    67	        except Exception:
    68	            continue
    69	        if not key[0] or not key[1] or not wall:
    70	            continue
    71	        groups.setdefault(key, []).append((run, float(wall), fp))
    72	    rows = []
    73	    for (pol, tr, hc), runs in sorted(groups.items()):
    74	        if len(runs) < 2:
    75	            continue
    76	        w = [x for _, x, _ in runs]     # wall_s（秒）——与论文别处用分钟不同，这里只算比例
    77	        spread = 100.0 * (max(w) - min(w)) / (sum(w) / len(w))
    78	        same_gen = len({fp for _, _, fp in runs}) == 1 and runs[0][2].strip("|")
    79	        rows.append(dict(policy=pol + ("+HC" if hc else ""), trace=tr, n=len(runs),
    80	                         same_code_gen="Y" if same_gen else "N",
    81	                         wall_min_min="%.2f" % (min(w) / 60), wall_min_max="%.2f" % (max(w) / 60),
    82	                         spread_pct="%.1f" % spread,
    83	                         runs=" ".join(r for r, _, _ in runs)))
    84	    rows.sort(key=lambda r: -float(r["spread_pct"]))
    85	    print("%-42s %-30s %3s %8s  %s" % ("policy", "trace", "n", "散布%", "runs"))
    86	    for r in rows:
    87	        print("%-42s %-30s %3d %8s  %s" % (r["policy"][:42], r["trace"][:30], r["n"],
    88	                                           r["spread_pct"], r["runs"][:60]))
    89	    sp = [float(r["spread_pct"]) for r in rows]
    90	    hdr = ["# 同 policy + 同 trace 的重复 run 之间的墙钟散布 —— gen_repeat_spread.py 生成（实验机上跑）",
    91	           "# 判据：同 policy + 同 trace 且落地 >=2 条；散布 = (max-min)/mean，单位 %。",
    92	           "# 论文 §VI 引的上限（跨配置与档位）就是本表的 max；全表 %d 组，min %.1f%%、max %.1f%%。"
    93	           % (len(rows), min(sp), max(sp)),
    94	           "# 分组键 = policy + trace + HiCache，只算【跑完的】run；same_code_gen 标注该组是否同代码代",
    95	           "#   （代码在两次之间会变，所以精确同代的分组太稀疏 —— 上界因此含少量代码漂移）。",
    96	           "# 不进表：被争用作废的旧测量（%s）—— 它与 _r2 同 policy 但不同类，" % " ".join(sorted(SUPERSEDED)),
    97	           "policy,trace,n,same_code_gen,wall_min_min,wall_min_max,spread_pct,runs"]
    98	    body = "\n".join(hdr) + "\n" + "\n".join(
    99	        "%s,%s,%d,%s,%s,%s,%s,\"%s\"" % (r["policy"], r["trace"], r["n"], r["same_code_gen"],
   100	                                         r["wall_min_min"], r["wall_min_max"],
   101	                                         r["spread_pct"], r["runs"]) for r in rows) + "\n"
   102	    assert isinstance(body, str)
   103	    with open(out_p, "w") as f:
   104	        f.write(body)
   105	    print("-> %s（%d 组；min %.1f%% max %.1f%%）；跳过 %d 条（未跑完或无 code 块）"
   106	          % (out_p, len(rows), min(sp), max(sp), len(skipped)))
   107	
   108	
   109	if __name__ == "__main__":
   110	    main()

    61	def contested_runs():

    78	def fig_frontier():

   152	# ------------------------------------------------------- 图 2：墙钟两段分解
   153	def fig_decomp():
   154	    """C1/C3：墙钟 = 有效工作 + 空转；空转 7.4%–36.3%。
   155	
   156	    柱长是【采样跨度】span（busy+idle 精确等于它），不是 run_summary 的墙钟；

   163	    idle = [float(r["idle_min"]) for r in rows]

   176	    ax.invert_yaxis()

   190	# --------------------------------------------------- 图 3：步时间的两个测量
   191	def fig_steptime():
   192	    """C2：步时间由两个【独立】方法测到，且两者给出一致的排序。
   193	
   194	    `metrics.csv` 的 itl/e2e/ttft 列全是空的，所以这不是引擎直采：
   195	    方法一是从 busy 时间反推的导出量，方法二是逐请求的 decode 间隔。
   196	
   197	    画成哑铃图而不是散点图，有两个理由：
   198	      1. 13 条 run 各有两个测量，散点图的文字标注必然互相压住；
   199	      2. 这张图要证的是【排序一致】。按方法一排序、把方法二画在旁边，
   200	         如果两组标记的纵向次序基本相同，一致性就是看出来的。实测 Spearman
   201	         rho=0.967，剩下的分歧都是步时间相差 2ms 以内的相邻互换。
   202	    ⚠ 步时间【不】随并发单调（cap4_hc 并发 1.60 → 25.7ms，F:hz0 并发 1.41 →
   203	      33.1ms），所以横轴不再是并发。图只报测量，不给因果。
   204	    """

   215	    ax.scatter([float(r["step_perreq_ms"]) for r in rows], y, marker="x", s=22,

   217	               label="per-request (independent)")
   218	    _bad = contested_runs()

   227	    ax.text(16.8, len(rows) - 0.55, "weights-read floor\n(15.2 GB / 936 GB/s)",
   228	            fontsize=6, color="0.35", va="bottom", linespacing=1.25)
   229	    legend_below(ax, ncol=1, y=-0.16)
   230	    fig.savefig(os.path.join(HERE, "fig3_steptime.pdf"))

 succeeded in 0ms:
   385	\subsection{Route 1: the accounting base}
   386	
   387	Our controller's projection (Eq.~\ref{eq:proj}) charges a session for its full
   388	context even while it is parked in a tool wait, when its KV is evictable and
   389	therefore absent from the engine's reported occupancy. We argued this was a
   390	double charge and removed it, admitting on engine telemetry alone.
   391	
   392	The charge is not double, and removing it does what the argument predicted on
   393	the engine side: median occupancy rises from 49.2\% to 56.2\% of the pool and
   394	the busy-hour running count from 1.35 to 1.48, and the round-1+ prefix hit
   395	rate falls only from 80.6\% to 79.7\%, so no eviction spiral occurred. The run
   396	also finishes 25.4 minutes \emph{earlier} (265.9 against 291.3).
   397	
   398	It is nonetheless not a win. The SLO falls from 89.8\% to 85.9\%---3.90\,pp---
   399	and the static front itself falls 3.81\,pp over those same 25.4 minutes. The
   400	two runs therefore sit at the same distance below it: $-1.24$\,pp for the run
   401	that keeps the charge, $-1.33$\,pp for the charge-free arm, a 0.09\,pp
   402	difference against the 0.5\,pp spread the base configuration shows between its
   403	two runs. Removing the charge does not lift the controller above the envelope;
   404	it slides the operating point down along it, at the front's own slope.
   405	
   406	We want to be explicit that this is a revision. Our earlier measurement of this
   407	route had the arm 4.2 minutes \emph{slower} and 6.0\,pp worse, and we read the
   408	wall clock and the SLO as cancelling. That measurement was one of the
   409	co-tenancy-flagged runs (\S\ref{sec:repro}): its $\tau_2$ read 31.3\,ms where
   410	the same arm measured alone reads 24.8, and $\tau_2$ enters the busy time
   411	directly. Re-measured clean, the wall clock moves the other way---the arm
   412	trades 25.4 minutes for 3.9\,pp of SLO---so the cancellation was a property of
   413	the contamination, not of the mechanism. The occupancy half of the argument
   414	(49.2\% to 56.2\%) is a volume measurement and was never in doubt. We keep the
   415	route in the negative column, now on the stronger ground that the highest-leverage
   416	knob the mechanism argues for moves the arm along the front rather than over it,
   417	and we record the correction here rather than in a footnote because it is the
   418	one place in this paper where a re-run changed a conclusion's reasoning rather
   419	than its precision.
   420	
   421	We note that this claim had already been falsified once by source inspection:
   422	SGLang's \texttt{token\_usage} subtracts evictable blocks, so a parked
   423	session's prefix is genuinely not counted, and charging for it reserves
   424	capacity that really will be needed back. The end-to-end measurement agrees
   425	with the source reading.

   428	all-samples mean, where the ordering happens to run the other way;
   429	\texttt{arm\_running.csv} carries both conventions side by side, and we name
   430	the one each claim uses.) The weaker statement that survives is the one we use---%
   431	the stretches are structural and long.
   432	
   433	The obvious remedy---keep more sessions live so that someone is always
   434	ready---is what \texttt{bres} does. It works in the narrow sense: \texttt{bres}
   435	recovers about 17 of the minutes that its own baseline spends idle. It is
   436	still slower overall, because the sessions it keeps live raise the step cost
   437	by more than the recovered idle is worth---and, as above, that step-cost rise
   438	is measured under co-tenancy and is therefore an upper bound. Idle is not unrecoverable; it is
   439	recoverable at a price that is not worth paying here.
   440	The structural remedy would be to make the engine do work during a wait, but
   441	there is no work to do: the session's next round does not exist until its tool
   442	returns.

   105	  (\texttt{runs\_precise.csv} records the step count of each run), over a
   106	  window up to four steps shorter.
   107	  $^\ddagger$ marks a run measured while three or more other engine
   108	  instances shared the host for at least an hour. That is a risk flag, not a
   109	  measurement of damage, and the two come apart in our data. Four flagged
   110	  rows---\texttt{horizon}$=2$, \texttt{horizon}$=0$, \texttt{horizon}$=1$ no
   111	  hw, and the resident base---are controller variants whose per-request
   112	  decode interval is 30.9--33.3\,ms against 23.8--25.7 for the same family
   113	  measured alone, so their wall clock, SLO and $\Delta$ are not comparable
   114	  with the rest. Three of the four are being re-measured; the fourth, the
   115	  resident base, has been, and its row above carries the clean numbers
   116	  (24.8\,ms $\tau_2$, 25.4 minutes earlier, $\Delta$ $-$1.33 against the
   117	  base configuration's $-$1.24). The two remaining flagged rows, \texttt{cap1}
   118	  and the published-parameter AIMD row, show no aggregate inflation at all
   119	  (20.6 and 24.4\,ms), so we keep their numbers and say why.
   120	  \S\ref{sec:repro} gives the audit, the threshold and the evidence.}

   141	  \caption{Per-step cost by two independent measurements, one run per row:
   142	  circles are model-consistent (all engine steps), crosses are the per-request
   143	  decode interval. They do not agree---$\tau_1$ exceeds $\tau_2$ by 8--40\%---
   144	  and the gap is consistent with the prefill share, not measurement error;
   145	  \S\ref{sec:model} bounds that share rather than identifying it. The dotted line is
   146	  the cost of reading the weights once; every run sits above it. The six rows
   147	  marked $^\ddagger$ were measured under host co-tenancy, which is why they
   148	  cluster at the high end---their step times are the inflated ones
   149	  (\S\ref{sec:repro}).}
   150	  \label{fig:steptime}

    24	## 二、两条事实
    25	
    26	**1. `foyer_hc` 支配 `cap2`**：墙钟更短（297.6 < 310.5）**且** SLO 更高（89.8% > 89.5%）。
    27	草稿"在 SLO ≥85% 约束下最快"成立。**但只快 4.3%**——正好是跨天不可归因的量级，
    28	也正是配对实验要测的那一个数。
    29	
    30	**2. `cap4_hc` 把取舍本身消掉了。** `cap4` 快但丢缓存（0.459），`cap2` 保缓存但慢（310.5），
    31	而 **`cap4_hc` 命中率 0.681**（≈cap2 的 0.677、≈foyer_hc 的 0.697）**只要 219.0 分钟**。
    32	**HiCache 让"保缓存"和"提并发"不再互斥**——而且它是个**没有控制器的静态配置**。
    33	Foyer 剩下的唯一优势是 TTFT 那条轴（2228 vs 5473 ms）。
    34	
    35	## 三、门槛是选的，赢家随它翻
    36	
    37	```
    38	SLO ≥ 85%   →  候选只有 foyer_hc 与 cap2，foyer_hc 快 4.3%
    39	SLO ≥ 60%   →  cap4_hc 快 26%
    40	```
    41	
    42	同一份数据，换个门槛，"谁是赢家"翻过来。而 `SLO_MS = 10000` 这个常数和 `85%`
    43	这个门槛**都没有依据来源**。
    44	
    45	**所以框架问题的形状不是"我们的控制器快不快"，而是"服务等级定在哪"。**
    46	那是产品判断。定了门槛，赢家就定了。
    47	
    48	> ⚠ **不许做的事**：先看谁赢再选门槛。那是 R7 的同一类错误——
    49	> 用一个事后挑的口径去支持一个事先想好的结论。
    50	
    51	## 四、三条线各自买什么
    52	
    53	| | 主张 | 手里的证据 | 要花的卡时 |
    54	|---|---|---|---|
    55	| **A** 性能：Foyer 比 cap2 快 | "同 SLO 下我们的控制器更快" | **零条干净的**。4.3% 跨天不可归因 | 配对实验 ~20-40 卡时，**且队列顺序现在会破坏它自己的前提**（`pair_design` §五） |
    56	| **B** 性能：对 cap4_hc | 同上，但对手是真正的赢家 | **更少**。要赢 26% 或解释为什么不该赢 | 同上，需新写 lane |
    57	| **C** 测量/机制 | "我们测到了什么，以及十种会让测量说谎的方式" | **四条站着的断言 + 十条已记录的陷阱机制** | **0（数据已在盘上）** |
    58	
    59	## 五、C 线现在手里有什么
    60	
    61	**关于系统**：① 不治理的 agentic serving 在 24GB 卡上坍塌（命中率 1.6%、TTFT 中位 29.6 分、
    62	SLO 3.0%）；② 逐请求解码速率不随并发上升；③ 会话侧与引擎侧之间没有稳定换算系数
    63	（符号会翻转）；④ 216× 搬运比重算便宜。
    64	
    65	**关于装置**：池守卫、池子静默缩水、唯一一对同条件重复差 0.6%、总 token 离散度 0.76%。
    66	
    67	**方法学骨架（今天十次，同一族）**：差分错对象 → 相除错对象 → 统计量选错 → 仪器选错 →
    68	表比产物旧 → 流程破坏前提 → 验证同义反复 → 进程活着当成在写 → 在写当成写的是有用的 →
    69	**误删一条正确记录**。
    70	
    71	> **十次里没有一次是数据错或算错。** 全是"这个数 / 这张表 / 这个流程，
    72	> 已经不是我以为的那个东西了"。
    73	> 最后一条性质不同：前九次的代价是**我以为的不对**，第十次的代价是
    74	> **我把对的改成了错的**，而且没有机制会把它翻回来。
    75	
    76	## 六、机器现状
    77	
    78	```
    79	gpu0   gray 在用（492 MiB）—— 印证了卡 0/1 的 00:00-08:00 限制是对的
    80	gpu2   cap3_fixed，14:20 重试（原数据无效，与框架无关，无论如何都要跑）
    81	gpu3   rl90fix，约 16:20 落地
    82	```
    83	
    84	`cap3_fixed` 和 `rl90fix` **与框架决定无关**，无论选哪条线都该跑完。
    85	**只有在它们之后排队的配对 lane 取决于这个决定。**

   110	
   111	1. **步成本的分母**（τ 除以全时段均值却乘 busy 时间 —— 两个错误相互抵消，
   112	   让两种测量方法看起来一致）；
   113	2. `tool_wait` 中位 0.1s→**均值 16.85s**；
   114	3. oracle 单调性 / 增长敏感度那条结论（立在一个已被判无效的臂上）；
   115	4. 记账重复计费；
   116	5. 空转率随并发单调下降；
   117	6. 「驱逐→重算推高步时间」那次**错误的反驳**（被 `num_retracted_reqs≈0` 与
   118	   `prompt_tokens_total == 计划 prefill` 反驳 —— 反驳本身被反驳）。
   119	
   120	详见 `paper/claims_audit.md` 与 `docs/claim_register_20260921.md`。
   121	
   122	## 引用红线
   123	
   124	- arXiv **2608.30830** 自报对 MORI/Continuum 的 head-to-head 胜利**已被否证，禁引**；
   125	  它的 41% goodput 损失与 ~10× 复活差可以引。
   126	- **PrefixShield (2608.01657)** 已占 "admission responsibility" 术语，需避撞。
   127	- 任何引用的标题、作者、年份都必须核实过才能进 `refs.bib`。**不要凭记忆写文献。**
   128	
   129	## 硬件与运维
   130	
   131	- 平时只用**卡 2 和卡 3**。卡 0/1 仅限 00:00–08:00 且确认空闲（`memory.used < 200 MiB`）。
   132	- **别人占着卡就等，不要算剩余空间够不够。只在完整的卡上跑。**
   133	- **不要同时占满四张卡。** 卡 0/1 可用时，总并发仍应 <= 2 条车道。
   134	  2026-09-21 学到过一次（记在 `scripts/watchdog.sh` 注释里）：把四张卡全占满，
   135	  长跑进程会被共享机器上其它租户的 reaper 杀掉。**2026-09-24 又犯了一次**：
   136	  四条车道一起开，卡 0/1 的两条 run 在 02:36:46 / 02:37:00（相隔 14 秒，
   137	  同一绝对时刻）双双被 SIGTERM，只跑到 679 和 624 步就终止，两条都作废。
   138	  症状：`steps.jsonl` 里 `connection closed before message completed` 在某个时刻
   139	  突然成簇出现，`status=FAILED`；server 日志里 `Subprocess scheduler_0 crashed
   140	  with exit code -15`。**这个 SIGTERM 是结果不是原因，别去查谁发的。**
   141	- **2026-09-24 追加：四条车道就算没被杀，数据也已经废了。** 审计（`scripts/lab/gen_concurrency.py`
   142	  → `paper/data/concurrency.csv`）查明：同机同时 ≥4 条 run 且持续 ≥60 分钟，
   143	  会把**逐请求 decode 间隔中位 τ₂ 抬高 25~40%**（30.9~33.3 ms vs 同族干净值 23.8~25.7）。
   144	  2~3 条不抬（pairB_foyerhc 3 条跑 277 分钟，τ₂=23.8，正常）。
   145	  **危险之处：引擎侧所有指标都看不出异常** —— `num_running_reqs`、占用率、驱逐量、

## 机械核对

1. **MISMATCH（数字 MATCH，脚注不自洽）。** 表 I 的 `resident base` 是 265.9 / 2.41 / 85.9 / −1.33，已去掉 ‡；[table1.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/table1.csv:18) 对应未舍入值为 265.8658 / 2.41 / 85.8859 / −1.3312，状态为 `clean`。[表 I 正文](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:151)正确；但[脚注](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:109)仍把 `resident base` 称为“四条 flagged rows”之一，随后又说“另外两条 flagged”，混用了旧测量与当前表格。

2. **MISMATCH（总数 MATCH，脚注计数错）。** [concurrency.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/concurrency.csv:97) 中 `CONTESTED` 共 **14 条**，与[可复现性段](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:359)的“十四条”相符。当前表 I 带 ‡ 的是 **5 行**：`cap1`、`hz2`、`hz0`、`breshz1`、原参数 AIMD；旧 `bres` 仍在 14 条原始 run 中，但干净的 `bres_r2` 已替换其表格行。[脚注](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:107)把旧 `bres` 算进当前带旗行，因而不自洽。

3. **MATCH。** [table1.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/table1.csv:18)与[同表基线行](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/table1.csv:23)给出墙钟差 `291.2765−265.8658＝25.4107` 分、SLO 差 `89.7898−85.8859＝3.9039` 个百分点、前沿距离 `−1.2400` 与 `−1.3312`。[decomp.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/decomp.csv:20)也给出四舍五入后的 291.3 与[265.9](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/decomp.csv:26)。与 [Route 1](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:392)一致。

4. **MATCH。** [归因表正文](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:335)的 `bres` 56.2 / 1.481 / 29.5 / `unused mem.` 对上 [attribution.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/attribution.csv:12)。该 CSV 的 12 行数出：未用内存 3、可归因步成本 1、`τ high, not matched` 5、残差 3；与[正文汇总](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:359)一致。

5. **MATCH。** 以 [decomp.csv 的基线](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/decomp.csv:20)为参照，[default](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/decomp.csv:6) 的空转/忙时/墙钟为 −13.8 / +11.1 / −2.7 分；[nogate](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/decomp.csv:28)为 +10.1 / −29.2 / −19.0 分。与 [§III](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:37)一致。

6. **MISMATCH（两个墙钟口径被混引）。** [reuse_x.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/reuse_x.csv:14)可复算 Foyer 重复对的兑现差 **0.0332 点**，写 0.03 点正确。逐请求时间戳可复算 cap4 墙钟差约 **1.71%**、Foyer 约 **0.056%**，故正文的 1.7% / 0.06% 有原始日志依据；但它所引的 [repeat_spread.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/repeat_spread.csv:25)使用 `metadata.wall_s`，两行实际印成 **1.6%** 与 **0.0%**。该表确有两条 trace 的 **10 组**、范围 0.0–6.1%；[§VI](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_xtrace.tex:121)称“十对”也不精确，其中主 trace 的基线一组有 **4 条 run**，见 [repeat_spread.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/repeat_spread.csv:16)。

7. **MATCH。** [idle_pooled.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/idle_pooled.csv:6)为 1,844 段、均值 20.94 秒、17.5% 超过 20 秒、占空转时间 69.9%。我从 [idle_seg_raw.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/idle_seg_raw.csv:1)的 1,844 行重算：**322 段**超过 20 秒，即 17.462%；其时长占比 **69.909%**。与 [§III](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:23)一致。

## Major issues

- **已作废的结论仍留在 §III → 直接与干净重跑相反。** [§III 末段](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:433)仍称 `bres`“追回约 17 分钟空转，却因步成本上升而总体更慢”。干净重跑相对基线实际是空转 **−9.5 分**、忙时 **−15.5 分**、墙钟 **−25.4 分**，见 [decomp.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/decomp.csv:26)及[重跑记录](/root/paper/foyer/docs/finding_rerun_bres_20260925.md:50)。这是结论反向，不是舍入问题。

- **“九种变体均未越过前沿”的完整判决仍含三条不可比测量。** [表 I 脚注](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:110)和[可复现性段](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:383)自己声明 `hz0`、`hz2`、`breshz1` 的墙钟、SLO、Δ 不可用；[摘要](/root/paper/foyer/paper/main.tex:74)与[§V 开头](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_results.tex:10)仍给出覆盖全部变体的否定判决。现有干净子集支持“尚无测得的胜出者”，不支持对这三臂作最终判决。

- **“内存梯队决定”写成了因果结论，而关键对照仍未落地。** [摘要](/root/paper/foyer/paper/main.tex:48)、[§III 标题与结论](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:231)用 *decides/sets*；最接近的匹配对虽有相近占用率和在跑数，却在**不同日期、不同 GPU**运行，正文也承认同卡容量控制仍排队，见 [§III](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:304)。主 trace 的关联与 host 命中计数很有说服力，但尚不足以隔离“梯队”这一变量；第二条 trace 又全部是 device-only，见 [§VI](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_xtrace.tex:10)。

- **“静态 cap 胜过控制器”的判据依赖未测的时间片混合。** [§II](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_background.tex:87)承认没有运行静态 cap 的切换策略，队列和缓存跨切换可能改变线段；同处也写明最佳控制器相对最近的较快静态点反而高 **2.9 pp SLO**。因此“低于插值线”是清楚、可复算的结果，“被某条实测静态策略支配”则不是同一个结果。摘要和结论应始终保留这个限定。

## Minor issues

- [图 3 图注](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:146)仍称 **六**行带 ‡，当前应为五行；[图 2 图注](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:59)又说五条带旗柱的忙时“被抬高”，但其中 `cap1` 与 AIMD 被正文判为没有可测的整体抬升。
- [§III](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:114)把 `horizon=1` 重复 run 的“数字不可用”说得过宽：异常明确落在引擎 token 计数及其导出量；论文同时继续用该 run 的墙钟与 SLO 判读重复性。应限定不可用的列。
- **数字追溯缺口是“论文数据表缺列”，不是原始日志缺失。** §VI 的 1.7% / 0.06% 逐请求墙钟可由日志重算，但 [repeat_spread.csv](/root/paper/foyer/paper/data/repeat_spread.csv:25)没有该口径；[可复现性段](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_discussion.tex:371)所引 `cap1` 分时段 τ₂ 跳变也只见[审计记录](/root/paper/foyer/docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md:44)，不在 `paper/data/*.csv`。引用路径需要说准。

对照 [CLAUDE.md 的六条撤回](/root/paper/foyer/CLAUDE.md:109)，**没有发现六条中的任一条以原结论复活**：分母、工具等待均值、oracle 敏感度、重复计费、空转率单调性及错误的“驱逐不导致重算”反驳，正文均作了撤回或限定。§III 残留的旧 `bres` 解释是**另一个**未清掉的受损结论。

## 你会先攻击什么

我会先指向 [§III 的 `bres` 段](/root/paper/foyer/paper/sec_model.tex:433)：作者刚用干净重跑把该结论推翻，却在机制解释中原样保留。随后要求把三条仍受损的臂从最终判决中暂时分开，并把“梯队决定”的因果措辞降到现有对照能承受的强度。

**按现状投：我主观判断中稿率约 5–15%。这是审稿判断，不是预测。** 主要障碍是正文内部仍有一处反向结论，以及核心否定判决和因果措辞都越过了当前干净证据的边界。
tokens used
